AC(1-y) + (B(A-C))(1-y^2)/2 - B^2(1-y^3)/3.
But A-C = B + B y = B(1+y).
AC = A(A-B-B y)=A^2 - A B(1+y).
So H2(y)= A^2(1-y) - A B(1+y)(1-y) + [B^2(1+y)/2](1-y^2) - (B^2/3)(1-y^3).
Simplify: second = -A B(1-y^2). third = B^2/2 (1+y)(1-y^2)=B^2/2 (1 - y^2 + y - y^3). Combine first: A^2(1-y) - A B(1-y^2) + B^2/2(1 + y - y^2 - y^3) - B^2/3 + B^2 y^3/3.
Constant B^2: 1/2 -1/3 =1/6. y: -A^2 y + B^2/2 y. y^2: +A B y^2 - B^2/2 y^2. y^3: -B^2/2 y^3 + B^2/3 y^3 = -B^2/6 y^3. Constant A^2 + B^2/6. So H2(y)=A^2 + B^2/6 - (A^2 - B^2/2)y + (A B - B^2/2)y^2 - (B^2/6)y^3.
Check at y=0 should H1(1)=A^2 - AB/2 -B^2/6. Our H2(0)=A^2+B^2/6. Discontinuity? H1(1)=∫0^1 f(x)f(1-x)dx. H2(0) should same. Error in derivation. Need implement projection onto simplex efficiently:
`_project_simplex(v)`:
Given v length n. Want min ||x-v|| s.t sum x=1, x≥0. There is algorithm: sort v descending, find rho such that sum_{top}(v_t - θ)=1. Standard: u=sort(v desc); cw=cumsum(u); find largest k with u[k] - (cw[k]-1)/k >0; θ=(cw[k]-1)/k; x=max(v-θ,0). O(n log n). For n=10k, per Nesterov iteration sorting expensive (10k log ~140k) * many = heavy. We can use approximate clip normalize for speed; or implement linear-time? Sorting in numpy is C fast but 10k sort ~0.0005s? 1000 iterations=0.5s? Maybe okay. Actually 10k sort ~0.5ms, 10k transforms ~0.1ms; 1000 iter~0.6s. Fine. But subgrad FFT maybe ~1ms. Use exact projection in Nesterov; for L-BFGS use bounds no projection.

Alternatively use `_clip_normalize` for momentum; but projection important. We'll implement exact but maybe for large n use approximate if time.

Gradient LSE implementation with FFT for H:
```
def _lse_value_grad(a,beta):
  a=np.asarray float; n=len; S=sum; c=conv; d=n*c/(S*S); dmax=max; z=beta*(d-dmax); clip; e=exp; w=e/sum; L=dmax+log(sum_e)/beta; score=2L
  Cw = dot(w,c)
  H = _shift_sum_fft(a,w)
  grad = (4.0*n/(S*S))*H - (4.0*n*Cw/(S**3))*a   # since F=2L? Wait L gradient: 2 * [n/S^2*(2H? no) - ...]
```

def _mirror_step(a, beta_active, eta, t_blend):
    a normalize S=1
    c=conv
    d=c/cmax? use c directly because scale fixed. z=beta_active*(c-cmax); w=exp clip /sum
    H= shift_sum(a,w)
    Cw=dot(w,c)
    # KKT for t-form: H should be constant. We choose target λ = Cw (weighted mean by a) or total mean S? The mean of H over i = S*? ∑H_i =∑w_m ∑a_{m-i}=S. So unweighted mean =S/n=1/n. Weighted mean by a=Cw. Which constant from KKT? H_i=μ constant. With support all and sum1, ∑H=1 => μ=1/n. Since support all, unweighted mean sufficient. If some zeros, support unknown. At uniform, H=1/n, Cw=cmax=1/n, same. For general, weighted mean by a is more related to objective. Use λ = Cw.
    ratio = λ/(H+eps)
    # avoid extreme: clip ratio to e.g. [0.1,10], eta [0.1,1]
    new = a * exp(eta*log(ratio)) (or a*ratio^eta)

# To keep scale, add penalty sum-1 small.
def fun(x):
   x=np.maximum(x,0)
   S=sum(x)
   if S<1e-12: return 1e10, zeros
   val, grad = _lse_value_grad(x,beta)
   # Add penalty
   pen = 1e-5
   val_pen = val + pen*(S-1)**2
   grad_pen = grad + 2*pen*(S-1)
   return val_pen, grad_pen
bounds=[(0,None)]*n
res=minimize(fun,x,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,options={'maxiter':min(100,maxfun//2),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'tol':1e-8})
return score(res.x), normalize(res.x)
```

Let's detail `_mirror_optimize(a, active_beta, deadline, eta0, iterations)`:
```
def _mirror_optimize(a, active_beta, deadline, label="", initial_eta=0.3):
    a=normalize(a); best=a; best_sc=score(a); eta=initial_eta
    # Precompute? c/w each iter.
    for it in range(max_iters or until deadline):
       rem = deadline - now; if rem<1: break
       # maybe lower active beta over time? keep.
       c=_conv(a); dmax=max(c); z=active_beta*(c-dmax); w=exp clip /sum
       H=_shift_sum_dense(a,w)  # FFT if w dense; active_beta maybe many nonzero. Could use sparse topK if w has few >1e-8.
       Cw=dot(w,c)
       lam=Cw  # S=1
       eps=1e-12
       ratio = np.clip(lam/(H+eps), 1e-3, 1e3)
       # Try a few eta values
       accepted=False
       for s in [eta, 0.5*eta, 2*eta, 0.25*eta]:
           new = a * np.exp(s * (np.log(ratio)))  # ratio^s
           new = np.clip(new,0,None); S=new.sum(); if S<eps: continue; new/=S
           # blend? Instead try also t blend? Maybe direct.
           sc=score(new)
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               a=new; best_sc=sc; best=new.copy(); eta=min(2.0, eta*1.05); accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           eta *=0.7
           if eta<1e-3: break
       # sometimes blend with best
       if it%20==0 and a is not best? a=normalize(0.5*a+0.5*best)
    return best_sc,best
```

Projection exact:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    if n==1: return np.ones(1)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cst=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = u - (cst-1)/rho > 0
    # find last True
    k=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cst[k]-1)/(k+1)
    x=v-theta
    x[x<0]=0
    return x
```
Need v finite. For performance, maybe if all v positive and sum close1, clip normalize faster? But exact is okay. Could implement fast path: if min(v)>=0 and abs(sum-1)<1e-8, return normalize; else exact. Since updates often produce nonnegative. Use:
```
S=v.sum()
if S>0 and np.min(v)>=0 and abs(S-1)<1e-8: return v/S
```
But many calls want exact projection. We'll use exact but can use fast if no negatives and sum close? If sum not exactly1, clip normalize is not exact projection if values vary, but good. Maybe always exact to maintain.

`_lse_value_grad` should return finite gradient; handle sum small.
Stable LSE:
```
d = n*c/(S*S)
dmax = float(d.max())
z = beta*(d-dmax)
z = np.clip(z, -700, 700)
e = np.exp(z)
sum_e = e.sum()
w = e/sum_e
L = dmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta
score=2*L
Cw = np.dot(w,c)
H = _shift_sum(a,w)
grad = (4*n/(S*S))*H - (4*n*Cw/(S**3))*a
```
If beta small, w uniform over all M; L approximates? okay.

`_lbfgs_objective` top-level:
```
def _lbfgs_objective(x, args):
    beta, pen = args
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    S = x.sum()
    if S <= 0: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    # if some negative due L-BFGS bounds? bounds prevent; clip for safety
    if np.any(x < 0): x = np.clip(x, 0, None); S=x.sum()
    val, grad = _lse_value_grad(x, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100; grad=np.zeros_like(x)
    if pen > 0:
       penval = pen*(S-1.0)**2
       grad = grad + 2.0*pen*(S-1.0)
       val += penval
    return float(val), grad
```
Bounds 0,None; penalty small 1e-8 maybe. If x large due drift, score okay but gradient maybe. Use pen=1e-6 to keep sum near1.

L-BFGS options: `maxiter` and `maxfun`. Since maxfun counts function evals including gradient. Use `maxiter=min(maxfun, 200)`, `maxfun=min(maxfun, 800)`. For n=16384, maxfun maybe 150.

We can call L-BFGS at beta schedule. If it doesn't improve, skip. Use current best for next? The L-BFGS result may have score slightly worse; we can keep best but use result for continuation if score < best + small. We'll do:

def _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, k_active=3, tol=1e-4, max_no_improve=60):
   a=normalize(a); best=...; t=1; y=a; v=zeros; step=1.0; noimp=0
   lower = 1.0
   best_sc0=best_score
   while now<deadline-2:
      if deadline close break
      if nesterov:
         t_new=...
         beta_mom=(t-1)/t_new
         y = _project_simplex(a + beta_mom*v)
      else: y=a
      sc, H, Cw = _active_H_Cw(y, k_active, tol)
      if not finite: break
      d = H - H.mean()
      gd = -d
      # maybe if all d near zero break
      normd=np.linalg.norm(d); if normd<1e-15: break
      # Step: use dynamic: step = max(0.05, min(10, 0.1*sqrt(n)/normd?)? Let's calibrate.
      # We want step*normd ~ sqrt(n)*1e-4? For n=8192 sqrt~90, d norm maybe? If d norm~0.2? Hmm.
      # Use Barzily-Borwein with gap to lower:
      gap = max(best_score - 1.0, 0.01)
      step = gap / (max(normd,1e-8)**2) * 0.1
      step = min(50.0, max(0.01, step))
      # line search candidates
      accepted=False
      for s in (step, step/2, step/2, step*0.25, step*2):
          x = _project_simplex(y + s*gd)  # gd negative d
          sc_x = _score(x)
          if sc_x < best_score - 1e-12:
             a=x; best=x.copy(); best_score=sc_x; noimp=0; accepted=True; break
      if accepted:
          v=a-y
          t= t_new if nesterov else 1
      else:
          noimp+=1; step*=0.5; if noimp>... reset momentum (v=0; a=best; t=1); if step<1e-5 break
      if it%20==0 and not accepted: a=normalize(0.5*a+0.5*best)
   return best
```

def _lbfgs_lse(a0,beta,deadline,maxfun):

   base = int(max(20, min(maxfun, rem* (300.0/n)?)))

`_mirror_optimize` loops exact score each candidate. Need iterate until deadline. Use max? no.
Implementation:
```
def _mirror_optimize(a, deadline, active_beta, eta0=0.5, blend_period=20):
    a=_normalize(a); best=a; best_sc=_score(a); eta=eta0; noimp=0; it=0
    lower = 2*a.size/(2*a.size-1)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       rem=...
       c=_conv(a); cmax=float(c.max()); if cmax<=0 break
       z=active_beta*(c-cmax); z=clip; w=exp/sum
       H=_shift_sum(a,w); Cw=float(np.dot(w,c))
       lam=Cw
       if lam <= 0: break
       eps=1e-12
       ratio=np.clip(lam/(H+eps), 1e-3, 1e3)
       logr=np.log(ratio)
       improved=False
       # candidate steps in order: eta, 0.5eta, 2eta, 0.25eta, 1.5eta
       for s in (eta, 0.5*eta, 2.0*eta, 0.25*eta):
           new=a*np.exp(np.clip(s*logr,-10,10))
           new=np.clip(new,0,None); S=new.sum(); if S<1e-12: continue
           new /= S
           sc=_score(new)
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              a=new; best=new.copy(); best_sc=sc; improved=True; eta=min(2.0, eta*1.05); noimp=0; break
       if not improved:
           # maybe try blend with best
           blended = _normalize(0.5*a + 0.5*best)
           scb = _score(blended)
           if scb < best_sc: ...
           eta*=0.7; noimp+=1
           if noimp>30 or eta<1e-3: break
       it+=1
       if it % 50 == 0:
           # reset to best or blend to avoid drift
           a = _normalize(0.8*best + 0.2*a)
    return best_sc,best
```
This can be somewhat expensive: each iter 4 scores; if no improvement 30*4*active_betas maybe. But active_betas few. We can limit by deadline and maybe max iter? no.

Candidate steps: `eta` chosen from previous; use [eta, 0.5*eta]. If fails reduce. Good. Mirror may find improvements quickly; then many noimp? Could be okay. We'll allocate time slices per beta, not until total deadline. In `_full_optimize`, call mirror with sub_deadline; it stops when reaches.

```
def _full_optimize(a, deadline, label):
    best=_normalize(a); best_sc=_score(best)
    print start
    # LSE schedule
    # total time left = deadline - now; reserve 25% for subgrad final? We'll allocate proportional.
    rem_total=deadline - now
    lse_allocated = rem_total*0.35
    mirror_allocated=rem_total*0.30
    sub_allocated=rem_total*0.25
    final_reserved=rem_total*0.10
    lse_dead=now + min(lse_allocated, 600)
    betas=[500,2000,10000,50000,200000,500000]
    for beta in betas:
       if now>lse_dead-10: break
       sub=min(lse_dead, now+ (lse_dead-now)/(len(betas)-idx+1)) # not necessary
       maxfun based n
       sc,a2=_lbfgs_lse(best,beta,sub,maxfun)
       if sc < best_sc + 1e-8: best...
    # Mirror
    mirror_start=now; mirror_dead=min(deadline - rem_total*0.35, ...)
    for ab in [20,100,500,2000]:
       rem=mirror_dead-now; if rem<20 break
       sub=now + min(rem/4, 120)
       sc,a2=_mirror_optimize(best, sub, ab, eta0=0.5)
       update
       # quick subgrad after mirror?
    # Subgrad
    sub_dead=deadline - rem_total*0.10
    for k in [1,2,3,5,8]:
       rem=sub_dead-now; if rem<15 break
       sub=now+min(rem/5, 120)
       sc,a2=_subgrad_polish(best,sub,k,1e-5)
       update
    # final LSE high beta if time
    final_dead=deadline-5
    for beta in [500000,1000000,2000000]:
       if now>final_dead-15 break
       sc,a2=_lbfgs_lse(best,beta,final_dead, maxfun=50)
       update
    # final subgrad k=1 tight
    if now<final_dead-10:
       sc,a2=_subgrad_polish(best,final_dead,1,1e-7)
    return best_sc,best
```

We can solve h via projected subgradient:
Given a fixed, minimize Φ(h)=max_m conv(a,h) subject h≥0,sum1. This is convex. We can use LSE + accelerated gradient with line search. But solving h for each outer step costly. def _param_starts(n, rng, parent=None, cap=700):
 out=[]
 if parent: p=_resample(parent,n); add p, p[::-1]
 x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 _add uniform
 # ramps
 for slope in [-4,-2,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,2,4]: arr=np.maximum(1e-3, 1 + slope*x); add arr, arr[::-1]
 # exp
 for lam in [0.2,0.5,1,2,4,8,16,32]: arr=np.exp(lam*(x-0.5)); add, rev
 # powers
 for p in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2,5]: arr=(1-x)**p+1e-3; add,rev; arr=x**p+...
 # split/ratio
 splits=[0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7]
 ratios=[0.3,0.5,0.8,1,1.3,1.618,2,3,5]
 for s in splits: for r in ratios: arr=np.where(x<s,r,1.0); add, arr[::-1]
 # logistic smooth split: for s in splits, mu in [0.5,1,2,4,8]: arr=np.exp(mu*(s-x)); add, rev
 # quadratic samples
 for a in [-3,-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1,3]: for b in [-3,-1,0,1,3]: arr=1+a*x+b*x*x; if min> -? max; add if positive
 # sinus exp samples
 for k in [1,2,3]: for amp in [0.5,1,2]: arr=np.exp(amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x)); add, rev
 # mixture gaussians
 for centers in [(0.25,0.75),(0.2,0.8),(0.35,0.65)]: for w in [0.3,0.5,0.618]: for sigma in [0.1,0.2,0.4]: arr=w*np.exp(-((x-c1)^2)/(2*sigma^2))+(1-w)*np.exp(-((x-c2)^2)/(2*sigma^2)); add, rev
 # random smooth few
 for _ in range(10): freq.. add
 return out
```

Detailed code sketches:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    out[np.abs(out)<1e-15]=0.0
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)

def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return float('inf')
    a=a/S
    n=a.size
    c=_conv(a)
    if c.size==0: return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*n*c.max())

def _normalize(a):
   ...
```

idx = np.nonzero(cond)[0]

    k = n-1
else: k=int(idx[-1])
theta=(cst[k]-1)/(k+1)
x=v-theta; x[x<0]=0; return x
```

`_shift_sum_fft`:
```
def _shift_sum_fft(a,w):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    ar=a[::-1]
    AR=np.fft.rfft(ar,N)
    WR=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(AR*WR,N)[:3*n-2]
    h=conv[n-1:2*n-1].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    return np.maximum(h,0.0)
```

`_shift_sum(a,w)`: If w length not 2n-1? assume. Adaptive:
```
nz = np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-14)
if nz == 0: return zeros
if nz <= 32:
   idx, vals = np.nonzero(w > 1e-14)
   H=zeros; loop
else:
   H=_shift_sum_fft(a,w)
# clean tiny negative
H[H<0]=0
return H
```
Counting nonzero on dense w with small values; if beta moderate, many >1e-14; FFT. Good.

`_lbfgs_objective`:
```
def _lbfgs_objective(x, args):
    beta=float(args[0]); pen=float(args[1])
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x=np.nan_to_num(x,0,0,0); x=np.maximum(x,0)
    S=float(x.sum())
    if S <= 0: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    val, grad = _lse_value_grad(x,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100; grad=np.zeros_like(x)
    if pen != 0:
       delta=S-1.0
       val += pen*delta*delta
       grad = grad + 2.0*pen*delta
    return float(val), grad
```

`_lbfgs_lse`:
```
def _lbfgs_lse(a0,beta,deadline,maxfun):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    a=_normalize(a0)
    base=_score(a)
    rem=deadline-t0
    if rem<10: return base,a
    n=a.size
    if n>12000: mf=min(80,maxfun)
    elif n>6000: mf=min(120,maxfun)
    else: mf=min(200,maxfun)
    mf=int(max(20, min(mf, rem*2)))  # maybe too low for rem60 ->120
    opts={'maxiter': int(max(30, min(100, mf//2))), 'maxfun': int(mf), 'ftol':1e-13, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxls':20}
    try:
       res=minimize(_lbfgs_objective, a, jac=True, args=(beta,1e-6), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return base,a
    x=_normalize(getattr(res,'x',a))
    sc=_score(x)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc>1e6: return base,a
    if sc < base + 1e-10:
       # maybe return x (could be slightly worse than base? we allow)
       return min(base,sc), (x if sc<base else a)
    return base,a
```

`_subgrad_polish` code:
```
def _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, k_active=3, tol=1e-4, reset_after=30):
    a=_normalize(a); best=a; best_sc=_score(a); lower=2*a.size/(2*a.size-1)
    t=1.0; v=np.zeros_like(a); noimp=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
       if v.size==0: break
       t_new=(1.0+math.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))/2.0
       mom=(t-1.0)/t_new
       y=_project_simplex(a + mom*v)
       sc_y,H,Cw=_active_H_Cw(y,k_active,tol)
       if not np.isfinite(sc_y): break
       d=H - H.mean()
       normd=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
       if normd < 1e-14:
           it+=1; t=t_new; continue
       if normd < 0.1: step0=1.0
       else: step0=0.1/normd
       step0=float(min(50.0, max(1e-5, step0)))
       improved=False
       for s in (step0, 0.5*step0, 0.25*step0):
           x=_project_simplex(y - s*d)
           scx=_score(x)
           if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_sc - 1e-12:
               a=x; best=x.copy(); best_sc=scx; improved=True; noimp=0; v=x-y; break
       if not improved:
           noimp+=1; step0*=0.6
           if noimp > reset_after:
               a=best.copy(); v=np.zeros_like(a); t=1.0; noimp=10; step0=1.0
           if step0 < 1e-6: break
       it+=1; t=t_new
       if it % 50 == 0 and noimp > 10:
           a = _normalize(0.5*a + 0.5*best)
    return best_sc,best
```
Note `_active_H_Cw` normalizes y again; fine but extra conv. It computed c and H. The score `sc_y` maybe exact for y. We don't update best for y if y better? Could:
```
if sc_y < best_sc: best=y; best_sc=sc_y
```
Add.

def _perturb(a,rng,strength=None):
 a=_normalize(a)
 if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))
 x=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
 mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
 if mode0: q=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(a.size))
 elif mode1: t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)); q=(1-t)*a + t/a.size
 elif mode2: q=a*(1.0 + strength*0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(1,5)*x))
 elif mode3: q=a*(1.0 + strength*0.5*(x-0.5))
 elif mode4: idx partition random segments; q[a random]
 else: q=a.ravel...
 return normalize(clip)
```

def _select_starts(scored, max_count=3, min_dist=0.25):
   scored=sorted(scored)
   selected=[]
   for sc,a in scored:
      if len(selected)>=max_count: break
      ok=True
      for _,b in selected:
         if np.linalg.norm(a-b) < min_dist: ok=False; break
      if ok: selected.append((sc,a))
   # fill with top regardless
   for sc,a in scored:
      if len(selected)>=max_count: break
      if not any(np.allclose(a,b,atol=1e-12) for _,b in selected):
         selected.append((sc,a))
   return [a for sc,a in selected]
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    F(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2
using:
- asymmetric step/ramp starts (uniform is a local minimum),
- calibrated log-sum-exp gradient polishing,
- KKT-inspired mirror fixes for the active convolution peaks,
- projected subgradient polishing,
- multi-length exploration and final black-box verification.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()))


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def _resample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    m = a.size
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, a).astype(np.float64)


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cu = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    ok = (u - (cu - 1.0) / rho) > 0.0
    idx = np.nonzero(ok)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        k = n - 1
    else:
        k = int(idx[-1])
    theta = (cu[k] - 1.0) / float(k + 1)
    x = v - theta
    x[x < 0.0] = 0.0
    return x


def _shift_sum_fft(a, w):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    AR = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(AR * WR, N)[:3 * n - 2]
    h = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    h[h < 0.0] = 0.0
    return h


def _shift_sum(a, w):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    nz = np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-14)
    if nz == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if nz <= 32:
        idx, vals = np.nonzero(w > 1e-14)
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m, vm in zip(idx, vals):
            lo = max(0, m - (n - 1))
            hi = min(n - 1, m)
            minj = m - hi
            maxj = m - lo
            h[lo:hi + 1] += vm * a[minj:maxj + 1][::-1]
        h[h < 0.0] = 0.0
        return h
    return _shift_sum_fft(a, w)


def _lse_value_grad(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = _conv(a)
    d = n * c / (s * s)
    dmax = float(d.max())
    beta = float(max(1.0, beta))
    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
    z = np.maximum(z, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = e / se
    L = dmax + math.log(se) / beta
    score = 2.0 * L
    Cw = float(np.dot(w, c))
    H = _shift_sum(a, w)
    grad = (4.0 * n / (s * s)) * H - (4.0 * n * Cw / (s * s * s)) * a
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(score), grad


def _lbfgs_objective(x, args):
    beta = float(args[0])
    pen = float(args[1])
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    val, grad = _lse_value_grad(x, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
        grad = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if pen != 0.0:
        delta = s - 1.0
        val += pen * delta * delta
        grad = grad + 2.0 * pen * delta
    return float(val), grad


def _lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    base = _score(a)
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = float(deadline - now)
    if rem < 10.0:
        return base, a
    n = a.size
    if n > 12000:
        cap = 80
    elif n > 6000:
        cap = 120
    else:
        cap = 200
    mf = int(max(20, min(cap, maxfun, rem * 2.0)))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": int(max(30, min(100, mf // 2))),
        "maxfun": int(mf),
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxls": 20,
    }
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lbfgs_objective,
            a,
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta), 1e-6),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return base, a
    x = _normalize(getattr(res, "x", a))
    sc = _score(x)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e6:
        return base, a
    return sc, x


def _active_H_Cw(a, k_active, tol):
    a = _normalize(a)
    c = _conv(a)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    score = float(2.0 * a.size * maxc)
    active = np.where(c >= maxc - max(tol, 1e-12) * maxc - 1e-30)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if active.size > int(k_active):
        order = np.argsort(c[active])
        active = active[order[-int(k_active):]]
    k = float(active.size)
    Cw = float(np.mean(c[active]))
    H = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    wv = 1.0 / k
    for m in active:
        lo = max(0, m - (a.size - 1))
        hi = min(a.size - 1, m)
        minj = m - hi
        maxj = m - lo
        H[lo:hi + 1] += wv * a[minj:maxj + 1][::-1]
    H[H < 0.0] = 0.0
    return score, H, Cw


def _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, k_active, tol):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    t = 1.0
    v = np.zeros_like(a)
    noimp = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 1.0:
        t_new = (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t)) / 2.0
        mom = (t - 1.0) / t_new
        y = _project_simplex(a + mom * v)
        sc_y, H, Cw = _active_H_Cw(y, int(k_active), float(tol))
        if not np.isfinite(sc_y):
            break
        if sc_y < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_y
            best = y.copy()
        d = H - H.mean()
        normd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if normd < 1e-14:
            it += 1
            t = t_new
            continue
        if normd < 0.1:
            step0 = 1.0
        else:
            step0 = 0.1 / normd
        step0 = float(min(50.0, max(1e-5, step0)))
        improved = False
        for s in (step0, 0.5 * step0, 0.25 * step0):
            x = _project_simplex(y - s * d)
            scx = _score(x)
            if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = scx
                best = x.copy()
                v = x - y
                a = x
                improved = True
                noimp = 0
                break
        if not improved:
            noimp += 1
            step0 *= 0.6
            if noimp > 28:
                a = best.copy()
                v = np.zeros_like(a)
                t = 1.0
                noimp = 12
                step0 = 1.0
            if step0 < 1e-6:
                break
        it += 1
        t = t_new
        if it > 0 and it % 60 == 0 and noimp > 10:
            a = _normalize(0.6 * best + 0.4 * a)
    return best_sc, best


def _mirror_optimize(a, deadline, active_beta, eta0):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    eta = float(max(0.05, eta0))
    noimp = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 1.5:
        c = _conv(a)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        z = float(active_beta) * (c - cmax)
        z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
        z = np.maximum(z, -700.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            break
        w = e / se
        H = _shift_sum(a, w)
        Cw = float(np.dot(w, c))
        if Cw <= 1e-15:
            break
        eps = 1e-12
        ratio = np.clip(Cw / (H + eps), 1e-3, 1e3)
        logr = np.log(ratio)
        improved = False
        for s in (eta, 0.5 * eta):
            new = a * np.exp(np.clip(s * logr, -8.0, 8.0))
            new = np.clip(new, 0.0, None)
            ns = float(new.sum())
            if ns < 1e-12:
                continue
            new = new / ns
            sc = _score(new)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best = new.copy()
                a = new
                improved = True
                eta = min(2.0, eta * 1.05)
                noimp = 0
                break
        if not improved:
            noimp += 1
            eta *= 0.7
            blend = _normalize(0.75 * best + 0.25 * a)
            scb = _score(blend)
            if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = scb
                best = blend.copy()
                a = blend
                noimp = max(0, noimp - 3)
            if noimp > 26 or eta < 1e-3:
                break
        it += 1
        if it % 80 == 0 and noimp > 8:
            a = _normalize(0.8 * best + 0.2 * a)
    return best_sc, best


def _full_optimize(a, deadline, label=""):
    a = _normalize(a)
    cur = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    best_seq = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(30.0, float(deadline) - t0)
    print("[{}] start score={:.8f} n={}".format(label, best_sc, a.size))

    if total < 80.0:
        sc, seq = _subgrad_polish(cur, float(deadline), 2, 1e-5)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_seq = seq
        return best_sc, best_seq

    lse_dead = t0 + total * 0.38
    mirror_dead = t0 + total * 0.68
    sub_dead = t0 + total * 0.88
    final_dead = max(mirror_dead, float(deadline) - 12.0)

    # Smooth LSE polishing.
    betas = [300, 1500, 8000, 40000, 150000, 500000]
    idx = 0
    while time.monotonic() < lse_dead - 8.0 and idx < len(betas):
        beta = betas[idx]
        idx += 1
        rem_t = lse_dead - time.monotonic()
        if rem_t < 12.0:
            break
        sc, seq = _lbfgs_lse(cur, beta, lse_dead - 2.0, 160)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                print("[{}] LSE beta={:.0f}: {:.8f}".format(label, beta, best_sc))
            if sc < best_sc + 0.02:
                cur = seq.copy()
            else:
                cur = best_seq.copy()
        cur = _normalize(cur)

    # KKT-inspired mirror fixes with several active-set smoothings.
    active_betas = [20, 80, 300, 1200, 5000]
    idx = 0
    while time.monotonic() < mirror_dead - 8.0 and idx < len(active_betas):
        ab = active_betas[idx]
        idx += 1
        rem_t = mirror_dead - time.monotonic()
        if rem_t < 20.0:
            break
        sub = time.monotonic() + min(rem_t * 0.35, 180.0)
        sc, seq = _mirror_optimize(cur, sub, ab, 0.5)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                print("[{}] mirror beta={:.0f}: {:.8f}".format(label, ab, best_sc))
            if sc < best_sc + 0.02:
                cur = seq.copy()
            else:
                cur = best_seq.copy()
        cur = _normalize(cur)

    # Exact-peak subgradient polishing.
    for ka in [1, 2, 3, 5, 8]:
        rem_t = sub_dead - time.monotonic()
        if rem_t < 15.0:
            break
        sub = time.monotonic() + min(rem_t * 0.30, 160.0)
        sc, seq = _subgrad_polish(cur, sub, ka, 1e-5)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                print("[{}] subgrad k={}: {:.8f}".format(label, ka, best_sc))
            if sc < best_sc + 0.01:
                cur = seq.copy()
            else:
                cur = best_seq.copy()
        cur = _normalize(cur)

    # Final high-beta chase.
    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 25.0:
        for beta in [250000, 750000, 1500000, 3000000]:
            if time.monotonic() > final_dead - 12.0:
                break
            sc, seq = _lbfgs_lse(cur, beta, final_dead - 2.0, 70)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_seq = seq.copy()
                    print("[{}] final LSE beta={:.0f}: {:.8f}".format(label, beta, best_sc))
                if sc < best_sc + 0.01:
                    cur = seq.copy()
                else:
                    cur = best_seq.copy()
            cur = _normalize(cur)

    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 5.0:
        sc, seq = _subgrad_polish(cur, final_dead - 1.0, 1, 1e-7)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("[{}] final subgrad: {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))

    print("[{}] done best={:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))
    return best_sc, _normalize(best_seq)


def _quick_optimize(a, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_sc = _score(a)
    best_seq = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, float(deadline) - t0)
    if total < 40.0:
        sc, seq = _subgrad_polish(a, float(deadline), 2, 1e-5)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_seq = seq
        return best_sc, best_seq

    mid = t0 + total * 0.6
    for beta in [2000, 20000, 150000]:
        if time.monotonic() > mid - 10.0:
            break
        sc, seq = _lbfgs_lse(best_seq, beta, mid - 2.0, 80)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
            if sc < best_sc + 0.02:
                cur = seq.copy()
            else:
                cur = best_seq.copy()
        else:
            cur = best_seq.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 15.0:
        sc, seq = _mirror_optimize(cur, float(deadline) - 15.0, 200, 0.5)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
            if sc < best_sc + 0.02:
                cur = seq.copy()
            else:
                cur = best_seq.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 5.0:
        sc, seq = _subgrad_polish(cur, float(deadline) - 1.0, 2, 1e-5)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
    return best_sc, _normalize(best_seq)


def _perturb(a, rng, strength=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.28))
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    if mode == 0:
        q = a * np.exp(strength * 0.6 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        m = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        q = (1.0 - m) * a + m / n
    elif mode == 2:
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.5))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.8)) * strength
        q = a * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
    elif mode == 3:
        q = a * (1.0 + 1.5 * strength * (x - 0.5))
    elif mode == 4:
        q = a.copy()
        seg = max(4, n // 25)
        for _ in range(3):
            s0 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - seg)))
            s1 = s0 + seg
            q[s0:s1] *= float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.8))
    else:
        q = a * (1.0 + 0.35 * strength * np.abs(np.cos(np.pi * x)))
    return _normalize(np.clip(q, 0.0, None))


def _add_to_start_list(lst, arr, n, cap):
    if len(lst) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
        return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s > 1e-15:
        lst.append(arr / s)


def _param_starts(n, rng, parent=None, cap=500):
    out = []
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None:
        p = _resample(parent, n)
        _add_to_start_list(out, p, n, cap)
        _add_to_start_list(out, p[::-1], n, cap)

    _add_to_start_list(out, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n, cap)

    # Hard two-level steps are very strong asymmetric seeds.
    splits = [0.30, 0.38, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.62, 0.70]
    ratios = [0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 1.0, 1.30, 1.618033988749895, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0]
    for s in splits:
        for r in ratios:
            arr = np.where(x < s, float(r), 1.0)
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Smooth steps / exponential ramps.
    for s in [0.35, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.65]:
        for mu in [0.4, 0.8, 1.5, 2.5, 4.0, 8.0, 16.0]:
            arr = np.exp(mu * (s - x))
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Polynomial ramps.
    for slag in [-4.0, -2.0, -1.2, -0.8, -0.5, -0.3, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 2.0, 4.0]:
        arr = np.maximum(1e-3, 1.0 + slag * x)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Power laws.
    for p in [0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0]:
        arr = np.maximum(1e-3, (1.0 - x) ** p)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)
        arr = np.maximum(1e-3, x ** p)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Quadratic shapes.
    for qa in [-3.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0]:
        for qb in [-3.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 3.0]:
            arr = np.maximum(1e-3, 1.0 + qa * x + qb * x * x)
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Mild periodic modulations.
    for k in [1, 2, 3]:
        for amp in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
            arr = np.exp(amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
            _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Gaussian mixtures.
    mixes = [(0.25, 0.75), (0.20, 0.80), (0.35, 0.65)]
    widths = [0.12, 0.20, 0.32, 0.5]
    weights = [0.30, 0.50, 0.618033988749895, 0.75]
    for c1, c2 in mixes:
        for wgt in weights:
            for sig in widths:
                a1 = np.exp(-(((x - c1) / sig) ** 2))
                a2 = np.exp(-(((x - c2) / sig) ** 2))
                arr = float(wgt) * a1 + (1.0 - float(wgt)) * a2
                _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)
                _add_to_start_list(out, arr[::-1], n, cap)

    # Random smooth spectra.
    Nf = _next_pow2(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
    for _ in range(12):
        p = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0))
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) * n + 1.0) ** p)
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
        noise = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j * phase), Nf)[:n]
        noise -= noise.mean()
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.25))
        arr = 1.0 + amp * noise
        _add_to_start_list(out, np.maximum(arr, 1e-3), n, cap)
        _add_to_start_list(out, np.maximum(arr, 1e-3)[::-1], n, cap)

    # A few independent random starts.
    for _ in range(6):
        arr = 0.2 + 0.8 * rng.random(n)
        _add_to_start_list(out, arr, n, cap)

    return out


def _score_starts(starts):
    scored = []
    for arr in starts:
        sc = _score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, arr))
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    return scored


def _select_starts(scored, max_count, min_dist):
    selected = []
    for sc, a in scored:
        if len(selected) >= int(max_count):
            break
        ok = True
        for _, b in selected:
            if a.size == b.size and np.linalg.norm(a - b) < float(min_dist):
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            selected.append((sc, a.copy()))
    for sc, a in scored:
        if len(selected) >= int(max_count):
            break
        is_known = False
        for _, b in selected:
            if a.size == b.size and np.allclose(a, b, atol=1e-12):
                is_known = True
                break
        if not is_known:
            selected.append((sc, a.copy()))
    return [a for _, a in selected]


def _safe_eval(seq):
    try:
        if len(seq) < 2:
            return float("inf")
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, *args, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + float(budget_s) - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and p.size <= 65536:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        print("Parent available: len={}".format(parent.size))
    else:
        print("Parent unavailable.")

    # Initial exploration at 8192 and 4096. 8192 is a good balance between
    # continuous approximation and search speed.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    scored_8192 = _score_starts(_param_starts(8192, rng, parent, cap=450))
    scored_4096 = _score_starts(_param_starts(4096, rng, parent, cap=250))
    print("Init scoring done: {:.1f}s".format(time.monotonic() - t0))
    if scored_8192:
        print("Best init 8192: {:.8f}".format(scored_8192[0][0]))
    if scored_4096:
        print("Best init 4096: {:.8f}".format(scored_4096[0][0]))

    candidates = []

    if scored_8192:
        sel = _select_starts(scored_8192, 3, 0.22)
        for a in sel:
            candidates.append(a)
    if scored_4096:
        sel = _select_starts(scored_4096, 1, 0.22)
        for a in sel:
            candidates.append(a)
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(_normalize(_resample(parent, 8192)))
        candidates.append(_normalize(_resample(parent[::-1], 8192)))

    # De-duplicate candidates roughly by length + distance.
    uniq = []
    for a in candidates:
        b = _normalize(a)
        same = False
        for c in uniq:
            if b.size == c.size and np.linalg.norm(b - c) < 0.25:
                same = True
                break
        if not same:
            uniq.append(b)
        if len(uniq) >= 4:
            break

    if not uniq:
        uniq.append(np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_seq = uniq[0].copy()
    for c in uniq:
        sc = _score(c)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = c.copy()
    print("Initial best before main: {:.8f} len={}".format(best_score, best_seq.size))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    # Reserve time for longer-length polishing and final verification.
    reserve_final = min(850.0, max(260.0, rem * 0.40))
    main_dead = deadline - reserve_final
    if main_dead > time.monotonic() + 220.0:
        n_cand = len(uniq)
        if n_cand == 1:
            fracs = [1.0]
        elif n_cand == 2:
            fracs = [0.58, 0.42]
        elif n_cand == 3:
            fracs = [0.46, 0.32, 0.22]
        else:
            fracs = [0.40, 0.28, 0.20, 0.12]
        main_total = main_dead - time.monotonic()
        cum = 0.0
        for i, cand in enumerate(uniq[:len(fracs)]):
            cum += fracs[i]
            sub_dead = time.monotonic() + cum * main_total
            sub_dead = min(sub_dead, main_dead - 5.0)
            if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 20.0:
                break
            cn = cand.size
            if cn < 2048:
                cand = _resample(cand, 4096)
            elif cn > 16384:
                cand = _resample(cand, 16384)
            sc, seq = _full_optimize(cand, sub_dead, "traj{}".format(i + 1))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
        print("After main phase: {:.8f} len={}".format(best_score, best_seq.size))

    # Short random restarts from the current basin to avoid local minima.
    restart_end = min(deadline - 260.0, time.monotonic() + max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.15))
    if restart_end > time.monotonic() + 80.0:
        n_restarts = 3
        chunk = (restart_end - time.monotonic()) / float(n_restarts)
        for ri in range(n_restarts):
            rdead = time.monotonic() + chunk
            if time.monotonic() > rdead - 15.0:
                break
            p = _perturb(best_seq, rng, strength=0.16)
            sc, seq = _quick_optimize(p, rdead)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                print("Restart {}: {:.8f}".format(ri + 1, best_score))

    # Longer-length polishing at 16384, since the objective has a continuous
    # asymptote and longer sequences usually allow a flatter square-convolution.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 320.0:
        seq16 = _resample(best_seq, 16384)
        sc16 = _score(seq16)
        if np.isfinite(sc16) and sc16 < best_score + 0.02:
            print("Resampled to 16384: {:.8f}".format(sc16))
            best_score = min(best_score, sc16)
            best_seq = seq16 if sc16 <= best_score else best_seq

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
            sc, seq = _full_optimize(best_seq, deadline - 60.0, "long")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()

    # Final compact polish on the best length.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
        sc, seq = _full_optimize(best_seq, deadline - 15.0, "final")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    # Build a small final tournament and choose the best according to the
    # actual evaluator when possible.
    best_arr = _normalize(best_seq)
    candidates_final = []

    def _add_final(arr):
        if arr is None:
            return
        b = _normalize(arr)
        if b.size < 4:
            return
        if b.size > 32768:
            b = _resample(b, 16384)
        is_known = False
        for c in candidates_final:
            if b.size == c.size and np.allclose(b, c, atol=1e-10):
                is_known = True
                break
        if not is_known:
            candidates_final.append(b)

    _add_final(best_arr)
    _add_final(best_arr[::-1])
    _add_final(_resample(best_arr, 8192))
    _add_final(_resample(best_arr, 16384))
    for L in [1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384]:
        _add_final(np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))
    if parent is not None:
        _add_final(parent)
        _add_final(_resample(parent, best_arr.size))

    best_out = best_arr.copy()
    best_val = best_score
    internal_best = float("inf")
    internal_best_seq = None
    for cand in candidates_final:
        sc_int = _score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc_int) and sc_int < internal_best:
            internal_best = sc_int
            internal_best_seq = cand.copy()
        if len(candidates_final) > 1 and time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        ev = _safe_eval([float(z) for z in cand])
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            if not np.isfinite(best_val) or ev < best_val:
                best_val = ev
                best_out = cand.copy()
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0:
                break

    if internal_best_seq is not None and (not np.isfinite(best_val) or internal_best < best_val):
        best_out = internal_best_seq.copy()
        best_val = internal_best

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_out, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / s
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(z) for z in out_arr]
    try:
        final_ev = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        final_ev = best_val

    print(
        "FINAL internal={} verified={} len={} nodes={} time={:.1f}s".format(
            best_score,
            final_ev,
            len(out),
            best_arr.size,
            time.monotonic() - t_start,
        )
    )
    return out
```
